ENDSEM PRACTICE BANK — TWO SAMPLE-PAPER WORKFLOWS
==================================================

This is a question selector, not 640 extra code files. Each question points to
an existing standalone, menu-driven Python answer in 02_PICK_A_VARIANT.
The story names below are practice variations; the algorithm and operation
paths are actual ZIP entries checked while this bank was generated.

HOW TO USE
  1. Search this text for your story or choose any numbered question.
  2. Open the indicated Grant_Style or Voting_Style story ZIP.
  3. Follow its exact relative file path. Edit QUICK EDIT MAP labels, roles,
     example values, and the stated extra outputs. Save as a new .py name.
  4. Keep the operation folder. Merely renaming an output cannot switch
     Paillier addition into RSA/ElGamal multiplication or scalar scaling.

SHARED GRANT-STYLE REQUIREMENTS
  Three role menus: submitter, verifier, finance. One record has public ID,
  encrypted free-text description, encrypted numeric amount, record hash,
  signature, persistent storage, search by ID and decrypted description hex.
  Check signatures and hashes, combine only selected verified numeric values,
  decrypt only the final result, handle invalid input and modified records.

SHARED VOTING-STYLE REQUIREMENTS
  Three role menus: submitter, server, aggregate authority. One submission has
  a numeric value plus a group ID. Hash and encrypt payload, protect its key,
  sign it, timestamp it, verify and search without revealing individual values,
  aggregate verified encrypted values and release only the final result with
  authority shares. Store submissions across restarts and handle invalid input.
  Raw numeric-choice sums are sums of IDs, NOT candidate frequency counts.
  A per-choice histogram requires a ONE_HOT alternative and extra adaptation.

IMPORTANT BOUNDARIES
  The concrete operation folders handle sum, RSA product, ElGamal product,
  and Paillier scaled sum. A winning bid, maximum, comparison, average, tax,
  or tariff is additional logic; do not claim a total alone implements it.
  MD5/SHA-1/DES/3DES and textbook RSA multiplication are syllabus demos.
  Changing the file name makes a fresh data folder. Installing pycryptodome
  in the active interpreter is required for `from Crypto...` imports.

ALL CIPHER/HASH/SIGNATURE PERMUTATIONS
  Every story CORE has 25 cipher-and-mode folders:
    aes128/aes192/aes256/des/3des x cbc/cfb/ofb/ctr/ecb.
  Each has 26 valid hash/signature file choices:
    md5 or sha1 x {elgamal, rsa_pss, rsa_v15, schnorr}
    sha256, sha3_256 or sha512 x
      {dsa, ecdsa, elgamal, rsa_pss, rsa_v15, schnorr}.
  That is 650 CORE selections per shipped story. The three operation folders
  also contain their own matching cipher/hash/signature paths. Use the folder
  that exists for the required operation; some extra alternatives have their
  own key-transport/search/share axes. The 5 profiles below sample the matrix.
  To form any other supported question, replace the cipher/hash/signature
  portion of a listed path with an existing path in the same operation folder.
  Story labels and user input values are open-ended, so 'all imaginable'
  questions cannot be finite; this bank enumerates the syllabus-shaped flows.

SAMPLE PROFILE KEY
  P1 aes192_cfb / sha256 / rsa_pss
  P2 aes256_cbc / sha512 / elgamal
  P3 aes128_ctr / sha3_256 / ecdsa
  P4 3des_ofb / sha1 / schnorr
  P5 des_ecb / md5 / rsa_v15

QUESTION INDEX (each card is a complete prompt with shared requirements above)

### GRANT EXPENSE — Grant_Style, starting story 01_grant.zip
Q001 [P1] Grant expense. Build the shared Grant_Style workflow with Researcher / Reviewer / Finance Officer menus. Use Grant ID, a description, and Grant Amount.
     Algorithms: aes192_cfb, sha256, rsa_pss; operation: Paillier encrypted sum. Test numeric values 2 and 3; expected final value 2 + 3 = 5.
     Add outputs: "Submission stored" and "2 verified entries selected".
     OPEN: 02_PICK_A_VARIANT/Grant_Style/01_grant.zip / 01_CORE/aes192_cfb/sha256/rsa_pss.py
